   212	    if_neg (fun h => (Finset.notMem_erase p s) (Finset.mem_of_mem_erase h)),
   213	    Finset.erase_right_comm (a := p) (b := z₀),
   214	    Finset.insert_erase
   215	      (Finset.mem_erase_of_ne_of_mem hpz hps),
   216	    smul_smul, smul_smul, ← add_smul]
   217	  have hc := sgn_erase_cancel hps hz₀s hpz
   218	  have h2 := sgn_erase_self p (s.erase z₀)
   219	  have h3 := sgn_mul_self p (s.erase z₀)
   220	  have hcoef : sgn z₀ s + sgn p s * sgn z₀ (s.erase p) *
   221	      sgn p ((s.erase z₀).erase p) = 0 := by
   222	    rw [h2]
   223	    linear_combination sgn p (s.erase z₀) * hc - sgn z₀ s * h3
   224	  rw [hcoef, zero_smul]
   225	
   226	/-- Case `s \ A = {z₀}`, `p ∉ s`: the replacement case, via the
   227	contracting homotopy. -/
   228	lemma bdry_KGen_one_notMem {A : Finset V} {p : V} (hp : p ∈ A) {s : Finset V}
   229	    {z₀ : V} (hz : s \ A = {z₀}) (hps : p ∉ s) :
   230	    bdry (KGen A p s) = Kmap A p (bdryGen s) := by
   231	  obtain ⟨hz₀s, hz₀A⟩ := mem_of_sdiff_singleton hz
   232	  set u := s.erase z₀ with hu
   233	  have hpu : p ∉ u := fun h => hps (Finset.mem_of_mem_erase h)
   234	  have hsub₀ : u ⊆ A := by
   235	    rw [hu, ← Finset.sdiff_eq_empty_iff_subset, erase_sdiff, hz,
   236	      Finset.erase_singleton]
   237	  have hcone : coneGen p u = cone p (Finsupp.single u 1) := by
   238	    rw [cone_single, one_smul]
   239	  have hstep : bdry (cone p (Finsupp.single u (1 : ℤ)))
   240	      = Finsupp.single u 1 - cone p (bdryGen u) := by
   241	    have hhom := bdry_cone_add_cone_bdry p (Finsupp.single u (1 : ℤ))
   242	    rw [bdry_single, one_smul] at hhom
   243	    rw [eq_sub_iff_add_eq]
   244	    exact hhom
   245	  have hlhs : bdry (KGen A p s)
   246	      = sgn z₀ s • Finsupp.single u 1
   247	        - sgn z₀ s • cone p (bdryGen u) := by
   248	    rw [KGen_of_one hz hps, map_smul, hcone, hstep, smul_sub]
   249	  rw [hlhs]
   250	  conv_rhs => rw [bdryGen, map_sum]
   251	  have hterm : ∀ w ∈ s, Kmap A p (sgn w s • Finsupp.single (s.erase w) 1)
   252	      = sgn w s • KGen A p (s.erase w) := fun w _ => by
   253	    rw [map_smul, Kmap_single, one_smul]
   254	  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hterm, ← Finset.add_sum_erase _ _ hz₀s, ← hu,
   255	    KGen_of_subset hsub₀]
   256	  have hrest : ∀ w ∈ u, sgn w s • KGen A p (s.erase w)
   257	      = (sgn w s * sgn z₀ (s.erase w)) • coneGen p (u.erase w) := by
   258	    intro w hw
   259	    have hwz : w ≠ z₀ := Finset.ne_of_mem_erase hw
   260	    have herase : (s.erase w) \ A = {z₀} := by
   261	      rw [erase_sdiff, hz, Finset.erase_eq_of_notMem (by simp [hwz])]
   262	    rw [KGen_of_one herase (fun h => hps (Finset.mem_of_mem_erase h)),
   263	      smul_smul, hu, Finset.erase_right_comm (a := z₀) (b := w)]
   264	  have hconebdry : cone p (bdryGen u)
   265	      = ∑ w ∈ u, (sgn w u) • coneGen p (u.erase w) := by
   266	    rw [bdryGen, map_sum]
   267	    refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun w hw => ?_
   268	    rw [map_smul, cone_single, one_smul]
   269	  rw [Finset.sum_congr rfl hrest, hconebdry, Finset.smul_sum, sub_eq_add_neg,
   270	    ← Finset.sum_neg_distrib]
   271	  congr 1
   272	  refine Finset.sum_congr rfl fun w hw => ?_
   273	  have hws : w ∈ s := Finset.mem_of_mem_erase (hu ▸ hw)
   274	  have hwz : w ≠ z₀ := Finset.ne_of_mem_erase (hu ▸ hw)
   275	  rw [smul_smul, ← neg_smul]
   276	  congr 1
   277	  have hc := sgn_erase_cancel hz₀s hws (Ne.symm hwz)
   278	  rw [← hu] at hc
   279	  linarith [hc]
   280	
   281	theorem bdry_KGen (A : Finset V) {p : V} (hp : p ∈ A) (s : Finset V) :
   282	    bdry (KGen A p s) = Kmap A p (bdryGen s) := by
   283	  rcases Nat.lt_or_ge (s \ A).card 2 with h2 | h2
   284	  · rcases hc : (s \ A).card with _ | n
   285	    · exact bdry_KGen_subset
   286	        (Finset.sdiff_eq_empty_iff_subset.mp (Finset.card_eq_zero.mp hc))
   287	    · have h1 : (s \ A).card = 1 := by omega
   288	      obtain ⟨z₀, hz⟩ := Finset.card_eq_one.mp h1
   289	      by_cases hps : p ∈ s
   290	      · exact bdry_KGen_one_mem hp hz hps
   291	      · exact bdry_KGen_one_notMem hp hz hps
   292	  · exact bdry_KGen_two hp h2
   293	
   294	/-- ∂ ∘ K = K ∘ ∂: `Kmap A p` is a chain map. -/
   295	theorem bdry_Kmap (A : Finset V) {p : V} (hp : p ∈ A) (M : Chain V) :
   296	    bdry (Kmap A p M) = Kmap A p (bdry M) := by
   297	  induction M using Finsupp.induction_linear with
   298	  | zero => simp
   299	  | add f g hf hg => rw [map_add, map_add, hf, hg, map_add, ← map_add]
   300	  | single s c =>
   301	    rw [Kmap_single, map_smul, bdry_single, map_smul, bdry_KGen A hp s]

   304	
   305	lemma nrm_sum_le {α : Type*} (t : Finset α) (f : α → Chain V) :
   306	    nrm (∑ i ∈ t, f i) ≤ ∑ i ∈ t, nrm (f i) := by
   307	  induction t using Finset.cons_induction with
   308	  | empty => simp
   309	  | cons a t ha ih =>
   310	    rw [Finset.sum_cons, Finset.sum_cons]
   311	    exact le_trans (nrm_add_le _ _) (by omega)

   315	  · simp [hc]

**Per-Correction Verdicts**
1. Fix needed: `W nonzero` is an underclaim. Lean requires a witness `deg x' W ≠ 0`, not just `W ≠ 0`; see `lean/Taut/Zvol.lean:122-124`.
2. FAITHFUL. `lean/Taut/Splitting.lean` does not use `exists_cut`, `range_bd2_eq_ker_bd1`, `bd2`, or `gammaChain`; it does use `bdry_lk_eq_zero` at `lean/Taut/Splitting.lean:388`.
3. FAITHFUL. The split matches `eq_zero_of_supp_card_lt` and `eq_zero_of_closed_supp_card_eq`; the latter’s `1 ≤ k` is satisfied by `k = n+1`; see `lean/Taut/Projection.lean:383-396`.
4. FAITHFUL. The `(≤)` proof is exactly `Zvol_le` then `nrm_add_le`; no leftover no-cancellation claim in that direction; see `lean/Taut/Theorem1.lean:372-376`.
5. Fix needed: wrong citation placement. `hbU : nbhd y₀ (bdry U)=0` is proved by the coefficient argument at `lean/Taut/Splitting.lean:339-386`; `bdry_lk_eq_zero` only converts `hUnbhd` and `hbU` into `bdry (lk y₀ U)=0` at `lean/Taut/Splitting.lean:388`.
6. FAITHFUL. `Kkills_or_Kkills` is inclusive OR (`lean/Taut/Theorem1.lean:263-267`), the two norm bounds are used as stated (`lean/Taut/Theorem1.lean:408-412`), and the prime-step text no longer claims `IsTaut.splits`.

**Section Scan**
Definitions: mostly faithful, except items 1-3 below.  
§2 splitting/additivity: mostly faithful, except items 4-5 below.  
§3 theorems 3.1-3.3: faithful to the public endpoints and prime-step correction.  
§4 status table: faithful to listed endpoint names.  
§5 bridges: faithful; the PL/topological ball bridge is explicitly not formalized.

**Remaining Discrepancies**
1. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:58`: UNDERCLAIM. `Zvol` is defined for any chain, not only cycles (`lean/Taut/Zvol.lean:74-76`).  
Corrected sentence: “For any chain `X`, the filling volume is `Zvol(X)=min{||M|| : ∂M=X}` when such fillings exist; in this document it is used on cycles.”

2. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:81-83`: OVERCLAIM / definition imprecision. `IsSphere2` fields are purity, edge degree 2, connected vertex links, connected 1-skeleton, and Euler (`lean/Taut/Complex2.lean:97-102`). “Connected cycle” is not a field, though 2-regularity follows separately (`lean/Taut/Complex2.lean:236-239`).  
Corrected sentence: “`IsSphere2` means pure 2-dimensional, every edge lies in exactly two triangles, every vertex link is connected, the 1-skeleton is connected, and `V+F=E+2`; link 2-regularity is then proved from these hypotheses.”

3. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:133-135`: WRONG-CITATION / overformalization. There is no separate Lean predicate `RootedStickerball`; the formal any-root condition is `FreelyCleanShellable` (`lean/Taut/CleanShelling.lean:70-72`, `91-92`).  
Corrected sentence: “There is no separate Lean predicate `RootedStickerball`; ‘rooted at `t`’ is the corresponding existence clause in `FreelyCleanShellable`.”

4. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:247-248`: WRONG-CITATION. As in correction 5, the no-`y₀` boundary-neighborhood fact is the local `hbU` proof, not `bdry_lk_eq_zero`.  
Corrected sentence: “A coefficient computation proves `nbhd y₀ (∂U)=0` because the relevant faces lie in neither `X` nor `Y`; `bdry_lk_eq_zero` then converts this into `∂L=0`.”

5. `notes/taut-lean-proof.tex:252-254`: OVERCLAIM. Lean proves the split using literal filters `M.filter (· ⊆ A)` and `M.filter (¬ · ⊆ A)`; it does not prove these chains coincide with `K_{A,p}M` and `K_{B,q}M` (`lean/Taut/Splitting.lean:464-496`).  
Corrected sentence: “Thus every generator of `M` is `⊆ A` or `⊆ B`, so the literal restrictions give `M=M_A+M_B`; the boundary identities and tautness of the restricted pieces follow from the norm equalities and the `K`-map boundary estimates.”

3. FAITHFUL. The split matches `eq_zero_of_supp_card_lt` and `eq_zero_of_closed_supp_card_eq`; the latter’s `1 ≤ k` is satisfied by `k = n+1`; see `lean/Taut/Projection.lean:383-396`.
